keep as declared. ink 5 engines `node >=18`; our `>=20` floor is stricter — compatible.\n\n(none — every decision is resolved at plan time: blueprint ADRs D1–D6, plan ADRs D7–D9, and the\ndeps-audit resolutions above.)\n\n## Critical paths\n\nFor `/code-quality` D4 (mutation) when enabled: `src/chat-message.tsx` (role→glyph/color\nbranching) and `src/theme.tsx` (token merge/fallback logic) are the M0 critical paths.\n\n## Dependency Graph\n\n```\nPhase 0 (toolchain scaffold) ──▶ Phase 1 (theme stub) ──▶ Phase 2 (ChatMessage) ──▶ Phase 3 (bench + example)\n                                                                                          │\n                                                                                          ▼\n                                                                            Phase 4 (CI + language gate)\n                                                                                          │\n                                                                                          ▼\n                                                                            Final Phase (integration validation)\n```\n\nAll phases are sequential (each consumes the previous phase's outputs). No parallel tracks — the\nwalking skeleton is one vertical slice.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 0: Toolchain scaffold\n\n**Objective:** An installable, buildable, testable, lintable empty package with licensing and\nchangelog in place.\n\n### T0.1 — Package manifest, licensing, changelog (contract-test first)\n\n#### Objective\nCreate `package.json` (publishable shape), `LICENSE`/`NOTICE` (Apache-2.0), `CHANGELOG.md`\n(`[Unreleased]` seeded), protected by an executable manifest-contract test.\n\n#### Why this step (action + reasoning)\n\n1. **What:** Write the manifest contract test (RED), then `package.json` with the blueprint's\n   locked shape (ESM-only, types-first exports, react-only peer, ink dependency, engines\n   `>=20`), install devDependencies with pnpm, add Apache-2.0 LICENSE + NOTICE and CHANGELOG.md.\n2. **Why now:** Everything else (build, test, lint) hangs off the manifest; it must exist first\n   and be right first — ADR D8 makes it executable-contract-protected so later phases can't\n   silently corrupt it. Licensing lands in the FIRST commit touching distributable code\n   (Apache-2.0 is a roadmap compliance constraint); CHANGELOG discipline is Unbreakable Rule 6.\n\n#### Evidence\n- Exports/files shape: `knowledge-base/references/assistant-ui/packages/react-ink/package.json:16-31`.\n- Peer/engines posture: Blueprint §\"D1\" (ink ^5 dep, react-only peer, node >=20);\n  `knowledge-base/references/ink-ui/package.json:21,59`.\n- Version ranges: § Dependencies table (this plan).\n\n#### Files to edit\n```\ntests/package-manifest.test.ts — (NEW) RED contract test for packaging invariants\npackage.json                   — (NEW) manifest per ADR D1/D3/D7/D8\nLICENSE                        — (NEW) Apache-2.0 full text\nNOTICE                         — (NEW) \"Copyright 2026 Theo ecosystem contributors\" attribution\nCHANGELOG.md                   — (NEW) Keep-a-Changelog skeleton + [Unreleased] § Added entries\n```\n\n#### Deep file dependency analysis\n- `tests/package-manifest.test.ts`: reads `package.json` from disk (JSON import via `readFileSync`\n  — no build needed); no other file depends on it.\n- `package.json`: consumed by pnpm/tsup/vitest/eslint/CI; its `scripts` are the gate-chain\n  contract for T4.1; its `exports` is the integration-test import target in T2.2.\n- `LICENSE`/`NOTICE`/`CHANGELOG.md`: standalone; `CHANGELOG.md` is later verified by\n  `/implement`'s acceptance gate + `/review` hard gate.\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Manifest invariants (asserted by the test): `\"type\": \"module\"`; `exports` =\n  `{\".\": {\"types\": \"./dist/index.d.ts\", \"default\": \"./dist/index.js\"}}` exactly (single entry,\n  `types` key listed before `default` — required by Node/TS resolution order);\n  `\"files\": [\"dist\"]`; `\"sideEffects\": false`; `engines.node: \">=20\"`;\n  `peerDependencies` has EXACTLY the keys `react` (+ `@types/react` optional-peer is NOT added at\n  M0 — YAGNI); `license: \"Apache-2.0\"`; `name: \"@theokit/tui\"`; `private` ABSENT.\n- Scripts (contract for T4.1): `build` (tsup), `test` (vitest run), `test:watch`,\n  `typecheck` (tsc --noEmit), `lint` (eslint .), `format` (prettier --write .),\n  `format:check` (prettier --check .), `bench` (tsx benchmarks/run.ts),\n  `gates` (format:check && lint && typecheck && test && build — DoD order).\n- Edge cases: pnpm 10 requires `packageManager` field for corepack-strict CI — set\n  `\"packageManager\": \"pnpm@10.34.1\"` (dev machine version, evidence: local `pnpm --version`).\n\n#### Tasks\n1. Blueprint ADR D5 locks the flat-token shape; gemini-cli's taxonomy\n   (`semantic-tokens.ts:9-43`) is the naming source.\n\n#### Evidence\n- Context+hook consumption pattern: `knowledge-base/references/ink-ui/source/theme.tsx:63-68`\n  (`useComponentTheme` reads `useContext(ThemeContext)`).\n- Token taxonomy: `knowledge-base/references/gemini-cli/packages/cli/src/ui/themes/semantic-tokens.ts:9-43`\n  (text/status categories).\n- Deliberate M0 simplification (flat, no per-component styles): Blueprint §\"D5\".\n\n#### Files to edit\n```\nsrc/theme.test.tsx — (NEW) RED tests (fallback, passthrough, partial override)\nsrc/theme.tsx      — (NEW) TheoTheme type + defaultTheme + provider + hook\nsrc/index.ts       — add exports { TheoTUIProvider, useTheoTheme, defaultTheme, type TheoTheme }\ntests/export-surface.test.ts — extend: new exports asserted\n```\n\n#### Deep file dependency analysis\n- `src/theme.tsx`: no internal deps (domain layer); depends on `react` only (createContext,\n  useContext) — deliberately NOT on ink (tokens are renderer-agnostic).\n- `src/index.ts`: gains 4 exports; `tests/export-surface.test.ts` and (later)\n  `tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` are the callers.\n- `src/theme.test.tsx`: co-located per `rules/testing.md § 5` (`<name>.test.<ext>` JS/TS convention).\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Data structure (exact fields, per blueprint ADR D5 — role colors + status, nothing more):\n\n```pseudocode\ntype TheoTheme = {\n  role: {\n    user:      { prefix: string; glyph: string; text: string };\n    assistant: { prefix: string; glyph: string; text: string };\n  };\n  status: { error: string; success: string; warning: string };\n};\ndefaultTheme: user glyph \"> \" cyan, assistant glyph \"✦ \" magenta, text undefined-color-safe\nTheoTUIProvider({ theme?: PartialDeep<TheoTheme>, children }) -> merges over defaultTheme (shallow per top-level group — no deepmerge dep at M0)\nuseTheoTheme(): TheoTheme -> useContext(ThemeContext) ?? defaultTheme\n```\n\n- Invariants: `useTheoTheme()` NEVER returns undefined (fallback to defaultTheme — fail-safe\n  default, `rules/error-handling.md § 2` explicit over magic null); merge is shallow at the\n  top-level groups (`role`, `status`) — documented limitation, deepmerge arrives with M6 (YAGNI).\n- Edge cases: provider with `theme={undefined}` === no override; partial override of one role\n  keeps the other role's defaults (test asserts).\n\n#### Tasks\n1. Write the 4 RED tests in `src/theme.test.tsx`\n2. Implement `src/theme.tsx` minimal (parsimony rung 6)\n3. Export from `src/index.ts`; extend export-surface test\n4. expect(frame).toContain(\"◆ \")\nRED:     partial_override_preserves_untouched_token_groups() — override role.user only; expect(theme.status.error).toBe(defaultTheme.status.error); expect(theme.role.assistant).toEqual(defaultTheme.role.assistant)\nRED:     empty_theme_override_yields_default_tokens() — <TheoTUIProvider theme={{}}>; `wc -l src/theme.tsx` <= 500\n\n#### DoD (Definition of Done)\n- [ ] `pnpm gates` exits 0\n- [ ] CHANGELOG updated\n\n---\n\n## Phase 2: ChatMessage primitive\n\n**Objective:** `ChatMessage` renders user/assistant messages with role glyph + token colors,\nsnapshot-covered including NO_COLOR, integrated through the public entry.\n\n### T2.1 — ChatMessage component (snapshot + semantic tests first)\n\n#### Objective\nImplement `src/chat-message.tsx`: `ChatMessage({ role, children })` rendering\n`<Box>` + glyph prefix `<Text>` + content `<Text>`, colored via `useTheoTheme()`.\n\n#### Why this step (action + reasoning)\n\n1. **What:** RED tests — snapshot of `lastFrame()` per role, semantic `toContain` assertions,\n   NO_COLOR smoke test, and a shared `renderFrame` helper (one React tick); First production\n  consumer of the theme — proves the DIP seam.\n- `tests/helpers.tsx`: imported by `src/chat-message.test.tsx` and\n  `tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` (T2.2) — single determinism point.\n- `src/index.ts`: gains `ChatMessage`; benchmark + example are downstream callers (Phase 3).\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Signature:\n\n```pseudocode\ntype ChatMessageProps = { role: \"user\" | \"assistant\"; children: ReactNode };\nfunction ChatMessage({ role, children }): \n  tokens = useTheoTheme().role[role]\n  return <Box> <Text color={tokens.prefix}>{tokens.glyph}</Text> <Text color={tokens.text}>{children}</Text> </Box>\n\n# Example\ninput:  <ChatMessage role=\"user\">hello</ChatMessage>  (defaultTheme, FORCE_COLOR=1)\noutput: \"> hello\" with \"> \" cyan-colored (ANSI in snapshot), text default color\n```\n\n- Invariants: role is a closed union — TS exhaustiveness (`noUncheckedIndexedAccess` keeps\n  `theme.role[role]` total); component renders in ONE line for short content (wrapping is Ink's\n  concern, not asserted at M0 beyond the fixed-width snapshot).\n- Edge cases: empty children (renders glyph only — snapshot); NO_COLOR run must contain the same\n  visible text (glyph + content) with zero ANSI escapes (assert `\u001b[` absent).\n\n#### Tasks\n1. Depends on T1.1 + T2.1 exports; no production file changes.\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Asserts BOTH roles inside one provider with a custom theme override AND the defaultTheme path;\n  mirrors the smallest real consumer program (same shape as `examples/basic.tsx` in T3.2).\n- Edge case covered: nesting two ChatMessages under one provider (sibling render — Ink `Box`\n  stacking) — asserts both lines present in frame.\n\n#### Tasks\n1. CHANGELOG entry (test-only — grouped under the T2 entry)\n\n#### TDD\n```\nRED:     public_entry_composes_provider_and_message_for_both_roles() — import from '../src/index.js'; render provider(custom user glyph)+2 messages; expect(frame).toContain(customGlyph); expect(frame).toContain(\"✦ \"); expect(frame).toContain both contents (RED via intentionally-wrong import first, then GREEN with real path)\nGREEN:   Fix import to ../src/index.js — passes with no production change\nREFACTOR: None expected\nVERIFY:  pnpm vitest run tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx\n```\n\n#### Concurrency tests\n(none — single-threaded)\n\n#### Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `pnpm vitest run tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` exits 0 — imports resolve ONLY via `src/index.js`\n- [ ] Pass: quality — `pnpm lint` exits 0; `wc -l tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` <= 500\n\n#### DoD (Definition of Done)\n- [ ] `pnpm gates` exits 0\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: Benchmark + example (wiring triad closure)\n\n**Objective:** Render-benchmark harness with statistical protocol + committed baseline JSON +\nrunnable example — the milestone's runtime-observability pillar.\n\n### T3.1 — Benchmark harness + committed baseline\n\n#### Objective\n`benchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx` (workload) + `benchmarks/run.ts` (runner): renders a\n100-message thread + 300-token streaming append into the last message via ink-testing-library;\ncaptures frames + mean/peak/std-dev ms-per-frame over 1 warmup + ≥ 3 measured runs; writes\n`docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json` and prints a console table.\n\n#### Why this step (action + reasoning)\n\n1. **What:** Port react-ink's harness pattern to the M0 scale, upgraded with the statistical\n   protocol (≥ 3 runs, mean ± std dev) and JSON persistence.\n2. **Why now:** The cycle owner requires benchmark DATA as acceptance evidence; ADR D6/D9 lock\n   the harness design + data-only posture. It lands after ChatMessage exists (it renders it) and\n   before CI (which smoke-runs it).\n\n#### Evidence\n- Harness prior art: `knowledge-base/references/assistant-ui/packages/react-ink/benchmarks/run.ts:19`\n  (tsx spawn), `benchmarks/long-thread.bench.tsx:205-230` (frame-time sampling via\n  `performance.now()` + `stdout.frames` deltas), `:272-341` (console reporting).\n- Statistical protocol: `rules/analysis-golden-rule.md § 3` (≥ 3 runs, mean ± std dev, units +\n  methodology + hardware).\n- Artifact requirement: `rules/public-copy.md § 4` (reproducible benchmark artifact under\n  `docs/benchmarks/`).\n\n#### Files to edit\n```\nbenchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx — (NEW) workload + sampling + stats\nbenchmarks/run.ts                 — (NEW) discovers *.bench.tsx, spawns via tsx, forwards exit code\ndocs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json — (NEW) committed baseline (generated, then committed)\npackage.json                      — bench + bench:smoke scripts (declared in T0.1, verified here)\n```\n\n#### Deep file dependency analysis\n- `benchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx`: imports `{ ChatMessage, TheoTUIProvider }` from\n  `../src/index.js` (production caller — wiring pillar a) + `ink-testing-library` render.\n- `benchmarks/run.ts`: fs/child_process only; forwards non-zero exit (fail-loud —\n  `rules/error-handling.md § 2`).\n- `docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json`: consumed by future `/analysis` +\n  M1 regression comparisons; `.prettierignore`d (T0.2) so format never rewrites data.\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Workload: build 100 `ChatMessage` elements (alternating roles) under one provider; then 300\n  sequential `rerender()` calls appending a token to the last message's text (streaming\n  simulation, no timers — synchronous re-render loop keeps the bench deterministic-ish and fast).\n- Metrics per run: `frames` (stdout.frames.length delta), `mean_ms_per_frame`,\n  `peak_ms_per_frame`; sampled via `performance.now()` around rerender batches — honest\n  heuristic, same as prior art, noted in JSON `methodology` field.\n- Protocol: 1 warmup (discarded) + N=5 measured runs (exceeds the ≥ 3 floor); aggregate\n  `mean ± std_dev` per metric.\n- JSON schema (exact fields): `{ benchmark, date, node_version, hardware: {cpu, cores},\n  workload: {messages, streamed_tokens}, protocol: {warmup_runs, measured_runs},\n  runs: [{frames, mean_ms_per_frame, peak_ms_per_frame}], aggregate: {frames_mean,\n  mean_ms_per_frame: {mean, std_dev}, peak_ms_per_frame: {mean, std_dev}}, methodology }`.\n- `--smoke` flag: 1 measured run, NO file write — CI proof-of-execution (ADR D9).\n- Edge case: if `stdout.frames` yields 0 new frames for a batch (Ink coalescing), the sampler\n  skips the empty delta (prior-art behavior, `long-thread.bench.tsx:210` `if (newFrames <= 0) return`).\n\n#### Pseudo-code / Signatures\n```pseudocode\nasync function runOnce(): RunMetrics\n  instance = render(<App messages=100 />)         -- inside ink-testing-library\n  t0 = performance.now(); framesSeen = instance.stdout.frames.length\n  for token in 1..300:\n    rerender(<App messages=100 streamedTokens=token />)\n    sample(instance, now())                        -- frame-delta sampling\n  return { frames, mean_ms_per_frame, peak_ms_per_frame }\n\nmain: warmup(); runs = repeat(5, runOnce); writeJSON(aggregate(runs)) unless --smoke\n\n# Example aggregate output (real numbers land at implement)\n{ \"mean_ms_per_frame\": { \"mean\": 3.1, \"std_dev\": 0.4 }, ... }\n```\n\n#### Tasks\n1. glyph: string;\n  /** Color of the glyph prefix. */\n  prefix: string;\n  /** Color of the message text; `undefined` = terminal default color. */\n  text: string | undefined;\n}\n\nexport interface TheoTheme {\n  role: {\n    user: RoleTokens;\n    assistant: RoleTokens;\n  };\n  status: {\n    error: string;\n    success: string;\n    warning: string;\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Two-level partial override accepted by `<TheoTUIProvider theme>`.\n * Hand-rolled (no deepmerge dep at M0 — plan ADR D5); the merge is leaf-level\n * per role/status group (overriding one leaf preserves its siblings).\n * Recursive deepmerge is deferred to the M6 theme system.\n */\nexport interface TheoThemeOverride {\n  role?: {\n    user?: Partial<RoleTokens>;\n    assistant?: Partial<RoleTokens>;\n  };\n  status?: Partial<TheoTheme[\"status\"]>;\n}\n\nexport const defaultTheme: TheoTheme = {\n  role: {\n    user: { glyph: \"> \", prefix: \"cyan\", text: undefined },\n    assistant: { glyph: \"✦ \", prefix: \"magenta\", text: undefined },\n  },\n  status: { error: \"red\", success: \"green\", warning: \"yellow\" },\n};\n\nconst ThemeContext = createContext<TheoTheme>(defaultTheme);\n\nfunction mergeTheme(override: TheoThemeOverride | undefined): TheoTheme {\n  if (override === undefined) {\n    return defaultTheme;\n  }\n  return {\n    role: {\n      user: { ...defaultTheme.role.user, ...override.role?.user },\n      assistant: {\n        ...defaultTheme.role.assistant,\n        ...override.role?.assistant,\n      },\n    },\n    status: { ...defaultTheme.status, ...override.status },\n  };\n}\n\nexport function TheoTUIProvider({\n  theme,\n  children,\n}: {\n  theme?: TheoThemeOverride | undefined;\n  children: ReactNode;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <ThemeContext.Provider value={mergeTheme(theme)}>\n      {children}\n    </ThemeContext.Provider>\n  );\n}\n\n/** Never returns `undefined` — falls back to `defaultTheme` without a provider. */\nexport function useTheoTheme(): TheoTheme {\n  return useContext(ThemeContext);\n}\n","numLines":83,"startLine":1,"totalLines":83}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8c8cd94d-c35e-484b-be54-00f8428df49b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/paulo/Projetos/usetheo/theokit-tools/theokit-tui","sessionId":"56c33c5e-7528-4d27-b50f-010a5e093ccb","version":"2.1.187","gitBranch":"develop"}

Metrics: frame count + mean/peak ms-per-frame.\n15\t// Protocol: 1 discarded warmup + 5 measured runs, mean ± std dev.\n16\tconst N_MESSAGES = 100;\n17\tconst N_TOKENS = 300;\n18\tconst WARMUP_RUNS = 1;\n19\tconst MEASURED_RUNS = 5;\n20\t\n21\tconst smoke = process.argv.includes(\"--smoke\");\n22\t\n23\tconst tick = async (): Promise<void> =>\n24\t  new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 0));\n25\t\n26\tconst round = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000;\n27\t\n28\tinterface RunMetrics {\n29\t  frames: number;\n30\t  mean_ms_per_frame: number;\n31\t  peak_ms_per_frame: number;\n32\t}\n33\t\n34\tfunction App({ streamed }: { streamed: string }) {\n35\t  const history = Array.from({ length: N_MESSAGES }, (_, i) => (\n36\t    <ChatMessage key={i} role={i % 2 === 0 ? \"user\" : \"assistant\"}>\n37\t      {`message ${i} — the quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog`}\n38\t    </ChatMessage>\n39\t  ));\n40\t  return (\n41\t    <TheoTUIProvider>\n42\t      <Box flexDirection=\"column\">\n43\t        {history}\n44\t        <ChatMessage role=\"assistant\">{streamed}</ChatMessage>\n45\t      </Box>\n46\t    </TheoTUIProvider>\n47\t  );\n48\t}\n49\t\n50\tasync function runOnce(): Promise<RunMetrics> {\n51\t  const frameTimes: number[] = [];\n52\t  const instance = render(<App streamed=\"\" />);\n53\t  await tick();\n54\t  let lastSeen = instance.frames.length;\n55\t  let lastTime = performance.now();\n56\t  let text = \"\";\n57\t\n58\t  for (let i = 0; i < N_TOKENS; i++) {\n59\t    text += \"tok \";\n60\t    instance.rerender(<App streamed={text} />);\n61\t    await tick();\n62\t    const now = performance.now();\n63\t    const newFrames = instance.frames.length - lastSeen;\n64\t    if (newFrames > 0) {\n65\t      // Wall time distributed evenly across the frames flushed since the last\n66\t      // sample — same honest heuristic as the react-ink prior art.\n67\t      const perFrame = (now - lastTime) / newFrames;\n68\t      for (let k = 0; k < newFrames; k++) {\n69\t        frameTimes.push(perFrame);\n70\t      }\n71\t      lastTime = now;\n72\t      lastSeen = instance.frames.length;\n73\t    }\n74\t  }\n75\t  instance.unmount();\n76\t\n77\t  const frames = frameTimes.length;\n78\t  if (frames === 0) {\n79\t    // EC-2: never serialize NaN/Infinity aggregates from an empty sample.\n80\t    console.error(\"bench: 0 frames captured — aborting (EC-2 guard)\");\n81\t    process.exit(1);\n82\t  }\n83\t  const mean = frameTimes.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / frames;\n84\t  const peak = Math.max(...frameTimes);\n85\t  return {\n86\t    frames,\n87\t    mean_ms_per_frame: round(mean),\n88\t    peak_ms_per_frame: round(peak),\n89\t  };\n90\t}\n91\t\n92\tconst stats = (values: number[]): { mean: number; std_dev: number } => {\n93\t  const mean = values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length;\n94\t  const variance =\n95\t    values.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / values.length;\n96\t  return { mean: round(mean), std_dev: round(Math.sqrt(variance)) };\n97\t};\n98\t\n99\tconst fmt = (label: string, r: RunMetrics): string =>\n100\t  `${label.padEnd(12)} frames=${String(r.frames).padStart(4)}  mean=${r.mean_ms_per_frame\n101\t    .toFixed(3)\n102\t    .padStart(8)}ms  peak=${r.peak_ms_per_frame.toFixed(3).padStart(8)}ms`;\n103\t\n104\tconsole.log(\n105\t  `chat-message bench — ${N_MESSAGES} messages + ${N_TOKENS} streamed tokens` +\n106\t    (smoke ? \" [SMOKE: 1 run, no file write]\" : \"\"),\n107\t);\n108\t\n109\tfor (let i = 0; i < WARMUP_RUNS; i++) {\n110\t  const w = await runOnce();\n111\t  console.log(fmt(`warmup #${i + 1}`, w) + \"  (discarded)\");\n112\t}\n113\t\n114\tconst measured = smoke ? 1 : MEASURED_RUNS;\n115\tconst runs: RunMetrics[] = [];\n116\tfor (let i = 0; i < measured; i++) {\n117\t  const r = await runOnce();\n118\t  runs.push(r);\n119\t  console.log(fmt(`run #${i + 1}`, r));\n120\t}\n121\t\n122\tconst aggregate = {\n123\t  frames_mean: round(runs.reduce((a, r) => a + r.frames, 0) / runs.length),\n124\t  mean_ms_per_frame: stats(runs.map((r) => r.mean_ms_per_frame)),\n125\t  peak_ms_per_frame: stats(runs.map((r) => r.peak_ms_per_frame)),\n126\t};\n127\t\n128\tconsole.log(\"=== aggregate ===\");\n129\tconsole.log(\n130\t  `frames_mean=${aggregate.frames_mean}  ` +\n131\t    `mean=${aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.mean}±${aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.std_dev}ms  ` +\n132\t    `peak=${aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.mean}±${aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.std_dev}ms`,\n133\t);\n134\t\n135\tif (!smoke) {\n136\t  const baseline = {\n137\t    benchmark: \"chat-message-render\",\n138\t    date: new Date().toISOString(),\n139\t    node_version: process.version,\n140\t    hardware: {\n141\t      cpu: cpus()[0]?.model ?? \"unknown\",\n142\t      cores: cpus().length,\n143\t    },\n144\t    workload: { messages: N_MESSAGES, streamed_tokens: N_TOKENS },\n145\t    protocol: { warmup_runs: WARMUP_RUNS, measured_runs: measured },\n146\t    runs,\n147\t    aggregate,\n148\t    methodology:\n149\t      \"ink-testing-library render + rerender loop (one event-loop tick between \" +\n150\t      \"rerenders so Ink flushes); Metrics: frame count + mean/peak ms-per-frame.\n// Protocol: 1 discarded warmup + 5 measured runs, mean ± std dev.\nconst N_MESSAGES = 100;\nconst N_TOKENS = 300;\nconst WARMUP_RUNS = 1;\nconst MEASURED_RUNS = 5;\n\nconst smoke = process.argv.includes(\"--smoke\");\n\nconst tick = async (): Promise<void> =>\n  new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 0));\n\nconst round = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000;\n\ninterface RunMetrics {\n  frames: number;\n  mean_ms_per_frame: number;\n  peak_ms_per_frame: number;\n}\n\nfunction App({ streamed }: { streamed: string }) {\n  const history = Array.from({ length: N_MESSAGES }, (_, i) => (\n    <ChatMessage key={i} role={i % 2 === 0 ? \"user\" : \"assistant\"}>\n      {`message ${i} — the quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog`}\n    </ChatMessage>\n  ));\n  return (\n    <TheoTUIProvider>\n      <Box flexDirection=\"column\">\n        {history}\n        <ChatMessage role=\"assistant\">{streamed}</ChatMessage>\n      </Box>\n    </TheoTUIProvider>\n  );\n}\n\nasync function runOnce(): Promise<RunMetrics> {\n  const frameTimes: number[] = [];\n  const instance = render(<App streamed=\"\" />);\n  await tick();\n  let lastSeen = instance.frames.length;\n  let lastTime = performance.now();\n  let text = \"\";\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < N_TOKENS; i++) {\n    text += \"tok \";\n    instance.rerender(<App streamed={text} />);\n    await tick();\n    const now = performance.now();\n    const newFrames = instance.frames.length - lastSeen;\n    if (newFrames > 0) {\n      // Wall time distributed evenly across the frames flushed since the last\n      // sample — same honest heuristic as the react-ink prior art.\n      const perFrame = (now - lastTime) / newFrames;\n      for (let k = 0; k < newFrames; k++) {\n        frameTimes.push(perFrame);\n      }\n      lastTime = now;\n      lastSeen = instance.frames.length;\n    }\n  }\n  instance.unmount();\n\n  const frames = frameTimes.length;\n  if (frames === 0) {\n    // EC-2: never serialize NaN/Infinity aggregates from an empty sample.\n    console.error(\"bench: 0 frames captured — aborting (EC-2 guard)\");\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  const mean = frameTimes.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / frames;\n  const peak = Math.max(...frameTimes);\n  return {\n    frames,\n    mean_ms_per_frame: round(mean),\n    peak_ms_per_frame: round(peak),\n  };\n}\n\nconst stats = (values: number[]): { mean: number; std_dev: number } => {\n  const mean = values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length;\n  const variance =\n    values.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / values.length;\n  return { mean: round(mean), std_dev: round(Math.sqrt(variance)) };\n};\n\nconst fmt = (label: string, r: RunMetrics): string =>\n  `${label.padEnd(12)} frames=${String(r.frames).padStart(4)}  mean=${r.mean_ms_per_frame\n    .toFixed(3)\n    .padStart(8)}ms  peak=${r.peak_ms_per_frame.toFixed(3).padStart(8)}ms`;\n\nconsole.log(\n  `chat-message bench — ${N_MESSAGES} messages + ${N_TOKENS} streamed tokens` +\n    (smoke ? \" [SMOKE: 1 run, no file write]\" : \"\"),\n);\n\nfor (let i = 0; i < WARMUP_RUNS; i++) {\n  const w = await runOnce();\n  console.log(fmt(`warmup #${i + 1}`, w) + \"  (discarded)\");\n}\n\nconst measured = smoke ? 1 : MEASURED_RUNS;\nconst runs: RunMetrics[] = [];\nfor (let i = 0; i < measured; i++) {\n  const r = await runOnce();\n  runs.push(r);\n  console.log(fmt(`run #${i + 1}`, r));\n}\n\nconst aggregate = {\n  frames_mean: round(runs.reduce((a, r) => a + r.frames, 0) / runs.length),\n  mean_ms_per_frame: stats(runs.map((r) => r.mean_ms_per_frame)),\n  peak_ms_per_frame: stats(runs.map((r) => r.peak_ms_per_frame)),\n};\n\nconsole.log(\"=== aggregate ===\");\nconsole.log(\n  `frames_mean=${aggregate.frames_mean}  ` +\n    `mean=${aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.mean}±${aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.std_dev}ms  ` +\n    `peak=${aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.mean}±${aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.std_dev}ms`,\n);\n\nif (!smoke) {\n  const baseline = {\n    benchmark: \"chat-message-render\",\n    date: new Date().toISOString(),\n    node_version: process.version,\n    hardware: {\n      cpu: cpus()[0]?.model ?? \"unknown\",\n      cores: cpus().length,\n    },\n    workload: { messages: N_MESSAGES, streamed_tokens: N_TOKENS },\n    protocol: { warmup_runs: WARMUP_RUNS, measured_runs: measured },\n    runs,\n    aggregate,\n    methodology:\n      \"ink-testing-library render + rerender loop (one event-loop tick between \" +\n      \"rerenders so Ink flushes); { readFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\n\ninterface BenchBaseline {\n  benchmark: string;\n  date: string;\n  node_version: string;\n  hardware: { cpu: string; cores: number };\n  workload: { messages: number; streamed_tokens: number };\n  protocol: { warmup_runs: number; measured_runs: number };\n  runs: {\n    frames: number;\n    mean_ms_per_frame: number;\n    peak_ms_per_frame: number;\n  }[];\n  aggregate: {\n    frames_mean: number;\n    mean_ms_per_frame: { mean: number; std_dev: number };\n    peak_ms_per_frame: { mean: number; std_dev: number };\n  };\n  methodology: string;\n}\n\n// T3.1 (plan ADR D6/D9): the committed baseline is the milestone's\n// runtime-evidence artifact. EC-2: NaN is typeof number — Number.isFinite is\n// the real oracle against a corrupt baseline poisoning future regressions.\ndescribe(\"benchmark baseline (T3.1)\", () => {\n  it(\"benchmark_baseline_json_exists_with_statistical_protocol\", () => {\n    const baseline = JSON.parse(\n      readFileSync(\n        new URL(\n          \"../docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json\",\n          import.meta.url,\n        ),\n        \"utf8\",\n      ),\n    ) as BenchBaseline;\n\n    expect(baseline.protocol.measured_runs).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);\n    expect(baseline.runs.length).toBe(baseline.protocol.measured_runs);\n    expect(baseline.protocol.warmup_runs).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n\n    for (const run of baseline.runs) {\n      expect(run.frames).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n      expect(Number.isFinite(run.mean_ms_per_frame)).toBe(true);\n      expect(Number.isFinite(run.peak_ms_per_frame)).toBe(true);\n    }\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.frames_mean)).toBe(true);\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.mean)).toBe(\n      true,\n    );\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.std_dev)).toBe(\n      true,\n    );\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.mean)).toBe(\n      true,\n    );\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.std_dev)).toBe(\n      true,\n    );\n\n    expect(baseline.methodology.length > 0).toBe(true);\n    expect(baseline.node_version.length > 0).toBe(true);\n    expect(baseline.hardware.cores).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(baseline.workload.messages).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(baseline.workload.streamed_tokens).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n { Text } from \"ink\";\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\n\nimport { renderFrame } from \"../tests/helpers.js\";\nimport { TheoTUIProvider, defaultTheme, useTheoTheme } from \"./theme.js\";\nimport type { TheoTheme } from \"./theme.js\";\n\nlet captured: TheoTheme | undefined;\n\n// Reset via function call — keeps TS from narrowing `captured` to `undefined`\n// inside each test body (assignment-based narrowing would type it `never`).\nfunction resetCaptured() {\n  captured = undefined;\n}\n\nfunction Probe() {\n  captured = useTheoTheme();\n  return <Text>{captured.role.user.glyph}</Text>;\n}\n\ndescribe(\"theme stub (T1.1)\", () => {\n  it(\"use_theo_theme_returns_default_tokens_without_provider\", async () => {\n    resetCaptured();\n    const frame = await renderFrame(<Probe />);\n    // Ink trims trailing whitespace per line — assert the visible glyph char.\n    expect(frame).toContain(defaultTheme.role.user.glyph.trim());\n    expect(captured).toEqual(defaultTheme);\n  });\n\n  it(\"provider_passes_custom_theme_to_consumers\", async () => {\n    resetCaptured();\n    await renderFrame(\n      <TheoTUIProvider theme={{ role: { assistant: { glyph: \"◆ \" } } }}>\n        <Probe />\n      </TheoTUIProvider>,\n    );\n    expect(captured?.role.assistant.glyph).toBe(\"◆ \");\n  });\n\n  it(\"partial_override_preserves_untouched_token_groups\", async () => {\n    resetCaptured();\n    await renderFrame(\n      <TheoTUIProvider theme={{ role: { user: { glyph: \"$ \" } } }}>\n        <Probe />\n      </TheoTUIProvider>,\n    );\n    expect(captured?.role.user.glyph).toBe(\"$ \");\n    // Leaf-level merge proof: overriding one leaf preserves the sibling leaves.\n    expect(captured?.role.user.prefix).toBe(defaultTheme.role.user.prefix);\n    expect(captured?.status.error).toBe(defaultTheme.status.error);\n    expect(captured?.role.assistant).toEqual(defaultTheme.role.assistant);\n  });\n\n  it(\"provider_without_theme_prop_yields_default_tokens\", async () => {\n    resetCaptured();\n    await renderFrame(\n      <TheoTUIProvider>\n        <Probe />\n      </TheoTUIProvider>,\n    );\n    expect(captured).toEqual(defaultTheme);\n  });\n\n  it(\"empty_theme_override_yields_default_tokens\", async () => {\n    resetCaptured();\n    await renderFrame(\n      <TheoTUIProvider theme={{}}>\n        <Probe />\n      </TheoTUIProvider>,\n    );\n    expect(captured).toEqual(defaultTheme);\n
